lines:
  - id: f5695.h_zero

    expr: {op: param, ref: f5695.door_max}

  - id: f5695.h_doors_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit limit for all qualifying exterior doors ($500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5695.doors_max}

  - id: f5695.h_item_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit limit for any qualified energy property item ($600)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5695.item_max}

  - id: f5695.h_hp_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit limit for heat pumps and heat pump water heaters; biomass stoves and biomass boilers ($2,000)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5695.heat_pump_max}

  - id: f5695.h_sec_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section A may be completed: Yes on lines 17a, 17b and 17c, and No on line 17e"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line17a, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line17b, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line17c, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line17e, {const: false}]}
    note: "Open: the form says a Yes on line 17e allows the credit only for improvements not related to construction, while the instructions for line 17e say 'If you check the Yes box, skip lines 18a through 20d.' The door cost inputs do not separate construction-related costs. I followed the instructions: a Yes on 17e skips lines 18a through 20d."

  - id: f5695.h_doors_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Door credit allowed: Section A may be completed and a valid QMID is reported for each door"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_sec_a, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.qmid_doors, {const: true}]}
    note: "The instructions say a valid QMID must be provided for each item to qualify. A missing QMID is read as no credit for the doors."

  - id: f5695.h_19c_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 19a multiplied by 30% (0.30)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f5695.line19a, rate: {param: f5695.eehi_rate}}

  - id: f5695.h_19c_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 19a times 30%, not more than $250"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.h_19c_raw, f5695.h_door_max]}

  - id: f5695.line19c
    printed: "19c"
    label: "Multiply line 19a by 30% (0.30). Do not enter more than $250"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_doors_ok, {const: true}]}
          then: f5695.h_19c_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_doors_ok, {const: false}]}
          then: f5695.h_zero
    refuses: [f5695.business_use, f5695.joint_occupancy]

    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f5695.line28, f5695.line29h]}
    refuses: [f5695.h_joint_separate_homes, f5695.reconstruct_later, f5695.business_use, f5695.joint_occupancy]
    note: "On a joint return with separate main homes, the instructions call for a separate Form 5695 per home, with the combined amount on line 30; that case is refused. Part II costs connected with a reconstruction whose original use did not begin in 2025 are not paid in 2025, and the cost inputs do not separate them, so that case is also refused."

  - id: f5695.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Energy efficient home improvement credit. Enter the smaller of line 30 or line 31. Also include this amount on Schedule 3 (Form 1040), line 5b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.line30, f5695.line31]}

  - id: f5695.line32a
    printed: "32a"
    label: "If the special rule for joint occupants applies, check here and attach a statement"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f5695.joint_occupancy, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [f5695.joint_occupancy]
    note: "Joint occupancy allocation is not computed, so a Yes refuses. The box is therefore unchecked on every return the tool completes."

  - id: f5695.line32b
    printed: "32b"
    label: "If you live in a condominium or cooperative and have a fractional share of the qualified energy efficiency improvements or residential energy property expenditures, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f5695.condo, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.part1_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part I has an entry: clean energy property costs (lines 1 through 5b), fuel cell property, or a 2024 carryforward"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [f5695.line6a, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line7a, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f5695.carry_in, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The pages do not define this interface. I read it as 'Part I has something to report' (the instructions say to file the form even if none of the credit can be used): any line 1-5b cost, fuel cell property, or a carryforward."

  - id: f5695.part2_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part II yields an energy efficient home improvement credit amount before the tax-liability limit (line 30 more than zero)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f5695.line30, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The pages do not define this interface. I read it as Part II producing an amount on line 30, before the line 31 limit."

  - id: f5695.claiming_part1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Claiming the residential clean energy credit, Form 5695, Part I (line 13 more than zero)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f5695.line13, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Used for the Schedule 8812 Credit Limit Worksheet B condition. Line 13 is used rather than line 15, because line 14 depends on Form 1040 line 19, which can depend on Worksheet B; using line 15 would create a cycle."
```
